# Lessons-first queue (generated)

Generated by `node tests/scripts/ws-lessons-first-queue.cjs` from the school pacing and the skill records. Do not edit by hand: change the
sources (or the script) and re-run it; `--check` fails when this file is stale. The plan is `design/LESSONS_FIRST_PLAN.md`.

**Order:** round r = every grade's r-th unit by start week, K..5. **Unit:** one White Rose block as one school grade teaches it, plus the CCSS BUILD lessons the
school places in it. **CCSS lesson:** a lesson with a CCSS code and no "non-ccss" flag; each needs a short and a long lesson.
**Shape:** a heuristic proposal (LESSON_LIBRARY_PLAN §6 archetypes) until `design/lessons/SHAPES.md` (E4) decides it.
